# OmniRoute backlog integration ledger

Single tracking file for upstream PRs, issues, discussions, and local integration evidence.

## Status rules

- **Committed; validation pending**: a local commit references this ID. This does not prove full upstream parity, acceptance criteria, tests, or deployment.
- **Considered; unverified**: referenced in a saved worker report; source mapping and applicability still need verification.
- **Not reviewed**: present in the downloaded inventory, without recorded review evidence.
- **Reviewed; recommendation only** records an evidence-backed fix proposal, not an implementation or test result. **Reviewed; existing source cited, untested** records code/test paths found by review; no regression test was run.
- **Reviewed; deferred with reason**, **Blocked; specific gap/dependency**, **Duplicate; cross-reference recorded**, and **Rejected; item reason recorded** require item-level rationale/evidence in the linked review report.
- **Reviewed; post-integration recheck** marks a report pinned to an earlier source commit whose reviewed path overlaps a later integration; refresh before final disposition.
- Do not repeat completed review without a reason: changed upstream revision, failed tests, regression, or changed requirements. Pending validation is not duplicate review.
- Only mark **Implemented; verified** after source comparison and relevant passing tests are recorded.
- No deployment/reload is authorized. i18n remains last priority.

- **Rejected** requires a per-item reason and evidence; unreviewed, blocked, or low-priority items are not rejected. **Implemented; verified** requires the correction commit and passing test command/result, not merely a clean git diff.

## Coverage and evidence limitations

Earlier reports overstated completion and assigned incorrect meanings to some upstream IDs. Treat report summaries as leads, not verified facts. PR #15457, for example, concerns the Claude Code/OpenAI tool-history bridge, not a proven general canonical-JSON cache fix.

Current collected snapshot: 341 PRs, 338 non-PR issues, 519 discussions (1,198 unique records). All comment/review/reply manifests report complete evidence; collection does not mean substantive review. Historical ledger-only entries are retained, not counted in this snapshot. Prior category counts were keyword estimates, not coverage guarantees. Evidence and item-level reconciliation: `/home/ubuntu/_/omni/audit/coverage-reconciliation.json`; raw evidence: `/home/ubuntu/_/omni/audit/backlog/`.

Item-level review completed for the 1,128 pending captured records: 287 PRs, 322 issues, 519 discussions. Reports are under `/home/ubuntu/_/omni/audit/backlog-review/final-*-review.json`. Recommendations remain unimplemented unless a separate local commit/test row says otherwise. Source applicability was pinned to `8581c6e82d`; 47 reviewed rows whose paths overlap Codex commit `cbb9cbb091` are marked for post-integration recheck. Missing PR patches/review/check artifacts remain explicit in their reports.

The committed version pins, broad thinking-budget removal, quota scoring change, and model-family context-limit guesses require review before release. A commit alone is not a validated fix.

## Existing-commit audit: release blockers

Read-only source/evidence audit: `/home/ubuntu/_/omni/audit/committed-change-audit.md`. No tests were run by this audit. KEEP means retain for testing, not release-ready. Earlier claims of complete implementation were incorrect.

## Local changes without an upstream item mapping

| Commit       | Scope                                      | Status                                                                      |
| ------------ | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `eed359b42f` | CLI identity and Antigravity fallback pins | Committed; wire values and compatibility not fully verified                 |
| `62c63ad529` | Gemini-Web files copied from blue          | Committed; three wiring checks passed, full parity/integration not verified |
| `cbb9cbb091` | Codex transport, SSE and speed tiers       | Cherry-picked b1dffe40bd; focused checks pass; no deployment                |

- **Codex integration validation:** focused Node tests 60/60, dashboard UI tests 2/2; core/Open-SSE typechecks, changed-file ESLint, docs-sync, explicit-any, tracked-artifact gates pass. UI-key coverage meets the 80% floor in 41 locales; this is not a full translation pass. 100 synthetic SSE calls over 32 connections pass; this proves local scheduling only, not upstream account capacity. Dashboard typecheck reports the same 10 provider-detail/model-visibility diagnostics on base `8581c6e82d` and integration; no new diagnostics.

## Codex release follow-ups — pending (not implemented by `cbb9cbb091`)

- Add native session/thread identity isolation before introducing conversation-bound upstream WebSocket pooling.
- Implement reusable upstream WebSocket sessions for HTTP clients, incremental continuation, coordinated retry budgets, bounded buffers/backpressure, handshake cancellation, and explicit stage telemetry.
- Verify public API `stream_id` multiplexing on the ChatGPT-backed endpoint; currently unverified. Do not enable based on assumption.
- Verify tier eligibility per upstream account/model; do not infer entitlement from local catalog membership.
- Establish dedicated GPT-6/Ultrafast pricing and billing multipliers. Current estimates use legacy values and remain unverified; do not invent multipliers.
- Keep the app-server path disabled; optional investigation only.

## Outstanding operational investigations

- Increased Antigravity 403 responses: capture sanitized upstream error details and correlate provider, model, account, and endpoint. Cause not established.
- Cache-rate decline: distinguish request hit rate from token reuse; inspect denominator, failures/probes, account scope, and actual provider usage receipts. Cause not established.
- Preserve blue working-tree changes. No checkout, reset, deployment, or reload without the appropriate approval.
